<script>
(() => {
'use strict';
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
const phoneMQ = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 720px)');
const DPR = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
const ROMAN = ['I', 'II', 'III', 'IV', 'V', 'VI', 'VII', 'VIII'];
const URLF = s => 'url("' + s + '")';

/* ============================================================ seeded randomness + noise */
function mulberry32(a){ return () => { a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0; let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a); t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t; return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
function makeNoise(rand){
  const perm = new Uint8Array(512), base = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) base.push(i);
  for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--){ const j = Math.floor(rand() * (i + 1)); const t = base[i]; base[i] = base[j]; base[j] = t; }
  for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) perm[i] = base[i & 255];
  const grad = (h, x, y) => { switch (h & 7){ case 0: return x + y; case 1: return -x + y; case 2: return x - y; case 3: return -x - y; case 4: return x; case 5: return -x; case 6: return y; default: return -y; } };
  const fade = t => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
  return (x, y) => {
    const xf = Math.floor(x), yf = Math.floor(y), X = xf & 255, Y = yf & 255;
    x -= xf; y -= yf;
    const u = fade(x), v = fade(y);
    const aa = perm[perm[X] + Y], ab = perm[perm[X] + Y + 1], ba = perm[perm[X + 1] + Y], bb = perm[perm[X + 1] + Y + 1];
    const g1 = grad(aa, x, y), g2 = grad(ba, x - 1, y), g3 = grad(ab, x, y - 1), g4 = grad(bb, x - 1, y - 1);
    const l1 = g1 + u * (g2 - g1), l2 = g3 + u * (g4 - g3);
    return (l1 + v * (l2 - l1)) * .7;
  };
}
function fbm(N, x, y, oct){ let s = 0, a = .5, f = 1; for (let i = 0; i < oct; i++){ s += a * N(x * f, y * f); f *= 2.03; a *= .5; } return s; }
const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
function grainPass(ctx, W, H, amt, R){
  const img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H), d = img.data;
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4){ const n = (R() - .5) * amt; d[i] += n; d[i + 1] += n; d[i + 2] += n; }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
}

/* ============================================================ the eight generative works */
function genSunset(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  const hz = H * .57, sx = W * .63, sy = hz - H * .11, sr = W * .052;
  let g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, hz);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#2f2d5e'); g.addColorStop(.42, '#8a5487'); g.addColorStop(.78, '#e3815a'); g.addColorStop(1, '#f5bf6c');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, hz);
  g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, hz, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#b86d6b'); g.addColorStop(.35, '#4f4776'); g.addColorStop(1, '#1d2244');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, hz, W, H - hz);
  const landTop = x => { const t = x / W; if (t > .47) return 1e9; return hz - H * .21 * Math.pow(1 - t / .47, 1.25) - H * .025 * N(t * 7, 1.3); };
  const tx0 = W * .115, tx1 = W * .135, tTop = landTop(W * .125) - H * .13;
  const boat = { x: W * .34, y: hz + H * .1 };
  function col(x, y){
    const lt = landTop(x);
    if (x > tx0 && x < tx1 && y > tTop && y < lt + 3) return [262, 28, 22];
    if (Math.hypot(x - (tx0 + tx1) / 2, y - (tTop - H * .014)) < W * .012) return [52, 100, 84];
    if (y > lt && y < hz + H * .014) return [275 + R() * 30, 30, 15 + R() * 9];
    const bx = x - boat.x, by = boat.y - y;
    if (by > 0 && by < H * .08 && bx > -by * .55 && bx < 2) return [282, 32, 17];
    if (by > -H * .01 && by <= 0 && Math.abs(bx + W * .005) < W * .032) return [282, 32, 14];
    const ds = Math.hypot(x - sx, y - sy);
    if (y < hz){
      if (ds < sr) return [50, 100, 86 + R() * 8];
      const t = y / hz;
      let h = t < .45 ? lerp(245, 305, t / .45) : t < .8 ? lerp(305, 370, (t - .45) / .35) : lerp(10, 44, (t - .8) / .2);
      let l = lerp(30, 68, t), s = lerp(45, 82, t);
      const glow = Math.max(0, 1 - ds / (sr * 5.5));
      l += glow * 22; h = lerp(h, 45, glow * .7);
      if (N(x / W * 3, y / H * 15) > .2 && t > .25 && t < .9){ h = 330 + (R() - .5) * 24; l -= 9; s -= 8; }
      return [h, s, l];
    }
    const t = (y - hz) / (H - hz);
    let h = lerp(250, 236, t), l = lerp(46, 22, t), s = 44;
    const wv = Math.sin(y * .45 * (1 + t) * (330 / W) + N(x * .02 * (330 / W), y * .05) * 5);
    l += wv * 5;
    const cw = W * (.035 + t * .12) * (.7 + .3 * N(y * .08, 2.1));
    if (Math.abs(x - sx) < cw && wv > -.4){ h = 36 + R() * 12; s = 92; l = lerp(78, 52, t) + wv * 6; }
    if (t < .07){ h = lerp(22, h, t / .07); l += (1 - t / .07) * 12; }
    return [h, s, l];
  }
  const n = Math.round(W * H / 26), rad = W / 290;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++){
    const x = R() * W, y = R() * H;
    let [h, s, l] = col(x, y);
    h += (R() - .5) * 28; l += (R() - .5) * 14;
    const r = R();
    if (r < .08){ h += 180; s *= .8; } else if (r < .2){ h += R() < .5 ? -38 : 38; }
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${h.toFixed(0)},${Math.min(100, s).toFixed(0)}%,${Math.max(4, Math.min(95, l)).toFixed(0)}%)`;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, rad * (.75 + R() * .7), 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 420; i++){
    const a = R() * 6.2832, rr = Math.sqrt(R()) * sr * 1.05;
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${(46 + R() * 12).toFixed(0)},100%,${(80 + R() * 14).toFixed(0)}%)`;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx + Math.cos(a) * rr, sy + Math.sin(a) * rr, rad * .8, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  }
}

function genGrid(ctx, W, H, R){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efeadf'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const leaves = [], lines = [], minS = W * .13;
  (function split(r, d){
    const canV = r.w > minS * 2, canH = r.h > minS * 2;
    if (d > 4 || (!canV && !canH) || (d > 1 && R() < .2)){ leaves.push(r); return; }
    const vert = canV && (!canH || (r.w >= r.h ? R() < .7 : R() < .3));
    const f = [.3, .38, .5, .62, .7][Math.floor(R() * 5)];
    if (vert){
      const x = r.x + Math.max(minS, Math.min(r.w - minS, r.w * f));
      lines.push([x, r.y, x, r.y + r.h]);
      split({ x: r.x, y: r.y, w: x - r.x, h: r.h }, d + 1); split({ x, y: r.y, w: r.x + r.w - x, h: r.h }, d + 1);
    } else {
      const y = r.y + Math.max(minS, Math.min(r.h - minS, r.h * f));
      lines.push([r.x, y, r.x + r.w, y]);
      split({ x: r.x, y: r.y, w: r.w, h: y - r.y }, d + 1); split({ x: r.x, y, w: r.w, h: r.y + r.h - y }, d + 1);
    }
  })({ x: 0, y: 0, w: W, h: H }, 0);
  leaves.sort((a, b) => b.w * b.h - a.w * a.h);
  const fill = new Map();
  const whites = ['#efeadf', '#ebe5d8', '#f2eee4'];
  leaves.forEach(l => fill.set(l, whites[Math.floor(R() * 3)]));
  fill.set(leaves[0], '#cf2a1f');
  const mid = leaves.slice(2, Math.max(3, Math.floor(leaves.length * .5)));
  const small = leaves.slice(Math.floor(leaves.length * .5));
  if (mid.length) fill.set(mid[Math.floor(R() * mid.length)], '#1f3f96');
  if (small.length) fill.set(small[Math.floor(R() * small.length)], '#f0c02c');
  if (small.length > 2) fill.set(small[Math.floor(R() * small.length)], R() < .5 ? '#f0c02c' : '#161616');
  for (const l of leaves){
    ctx.fillStyle = fill.get(l); ctx.fillRect(l.x, l.y, l.w, l.h);
    ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(l.x, l.y, l.w, l.h); ctx.clip();
    const horiz = l.w > l.h;
    for (let k = 0; k < 70; k++){
      ctx.globalAlpha = .05 + R() * .06;
      ctx.fillStyle = R() < .5 ? '#ffffff' : '#000000';
      if (horiz) ctx.fillRect(l.x + R() * l.w * .3 - l.w * .1, l.y + R() * l.h, l.w * (.4 + R() * .7), .6 + R() * 1.6);
      else ctx.fillRect(l.x + R() * l.w, l.y + R() * l.h * .3 - l.h * .1, .6 + R() * 1.6, l.h * (.4 + R() * .7));
    }
    ctx.restore(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#141414'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .024; ctx.lineCap = 'square';
  for (const [x1, y1, x2, y2] of lines){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.stroke(); }
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 10, R);
}

function genColorField(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#3a0f18'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++){ ctx.fillStyle = R() < .5 ? 'rgba(96,22,34,.06)' : 'rgba(18,3,8,.07)'; ctx.fillRect(0, R() * H, W, R() * H * .07); }
  const blocks = [
    { y0: .055, y1: .52, a: [210, 62, 38], b: [238, 124, 60] },
    { y0: .57, y1: .612, a: [36, 8, 16], b: [60, 16, 27] },
    { y0: .66, y1: .945, a: [86, 28, 64], b: [124, 50, 88] }
  ];
  const x0 = W * .08, x1 = W * .92;
  for (const bl of blocks){
    const Y0 = H * bl.y0, Y1 = H * bl.y1, j = W * .014;
    for (let k = 0; k < 48; k++){
      const t = R();
      const c = bl.a.map((v, i) => Math.round(v + (bl.b[i] - v) * t));
      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},.075)`;
      const a0 = x0 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, a1 = x1 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, b0 = Y0 + (R() - .5) * j * 2, b1 = Y1 + (R() - .5) * j * 2;
      ctx.fillRect(a0, b0, a1 - a0, b1 - b0);
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < 280; k++){
      const y = Y0 + R() * (Y1 - Y0), t = R();
      const c = bl.b.map(v => Math.min(255, Math.round(v * (.8 + t * .45))));
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${c},${(.035 + R() * .05).toFixed(3)})`; ctx.lineWidth = 1 + R() * W * .012;
      ctx.beginPath(); const xs = x0 + R() * W * .08; ctx.moveTo(xs, y);
      const len = W * (.2 + R() * .62);
      for (let s = 0; s < len; s += W * .02) ctx.lineTo(xs + s, y + N((xs + s) * .012, y * .012) * H * .012);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  const rg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .5, H * .28, 0, W * .5, H * .28, W * .6);
  rg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,176,96,.2)'); rg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,176,96,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = rg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 12, R);
}

function genOp(ctx, W, H, R){
  const img = ctx.createImageData(W, H), d = img.data;
  const cells = 15, cs = W / cells;
  const cx = W * (.56 + (R() - .5) * .05), cy = H * (.44 + (R() - .5) * .05), R0 = W * .43;
  const A0 = 242, A1 = 238, A2 = 229, B0 = 17, B1 = 16, B2 = 20;
  const inv = 1 / R0;
  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++){
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++){
      let s = 0;
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++){
        const px = x + (k & 1 ? .75 : .25), py = y + (k & 2 ? .75 : .25);
        const dx = px - cx, dy = py - cy, r2 = (dx * dx + dy * dy) * inv * inv;
        let m = 1; if (r2 < 1){ const q = 1 - r2; m = 1 - .56 * q * q; }
        s += (Math.floor((cx + dx * m) / cs) + Math.floor((cy + dy * m * 1.04) / cs)) & 1;
      }
      const t = s / 4, i = (y * W + x) * 4;
      d[i] = A0 + (B0 - A0) * t; d[i + 1] = A1 + (B1 - A1) * t; d[i + 2] = A2 + (B2 - A2) * t; d[i + 3] = 255;
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 7, R);
}

function genInk(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe6d2'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  let pg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
  pg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(140,100,50,.04)'); pg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(140,100,50,.12)');
  ctx.fillStyle = pg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,96,60,.07)'; ctx.lineWidth = .6;
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++){ const x = R() * W, y = R() * H; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + (R() - .5) * W * .2, y + (R() - .5) * 6); ctx.stroke(); }
  const layers = 5;
  const inkAt = (t, a) => { const v = Math.round(22 + (1 - t) * 52); return `rgba(${v},${v},${v + 8},${a})`; };
  let nearRidge = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < layers; i++){
    const t = i / (layers - 1);
    const base = H * (.2 + t * .34), amp = H * (.09 + t * .05), len = H * (.07 + t * .09), a = .12 + t * .74;
    const peaks = []; for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) peaks.push({ x: R() * W, w: W * (.1 + R() * .22), h: .5 + R() * .7 });
    const ridge = x => { let v = .6 * N(x / W * 2.4 + i * 3.7, i * 1.3) + .3 * N(x / W * 7 + i, 5 + i); for (const p of peaks) v += p.h * Math.exp(-(((x - p.x) / p.w) ** 2)); return base - amp * v; };
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++){
      const top = ridge(x) + (R() - .5) * 1.3;
      const L = len * (.65 + .35 * N(x * .03, i * 9));
      const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, top, 0, top + L);
      g.addColorStop(0, inkAt(t, a)); g.addColorStop(.18, inkAt(t, a * .62)); g.addColorStop(1, inkAt(t, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, top, 1.3, L);
    }
    if (t > .3){
      ctx.strokeStyle = inkAt(t, .28 * t); ctx.lineWidth = W * .004;
      for (let k = 0; k < W * .35 * t; k++){
        const x = R() * W, y = ridge(x) + R() * len * .55;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + W * (.01 + R() * .02) * (R() < .5 ? -1 : 1), y + H * (.008 + R() * .012)); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    const mg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, base + len * .2, 0, base + len * 1.3);
    mg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(239,230,210,0)'); mg.addColorStop(.5, 'rgba(239,230,210,.7)'); mg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(239,230,210,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = mg; ctx.fillRect(0, base + len * .2, W, len * 1.1);
    if (i === layers - 1) nearRidge = ridge;
  }
  // foreground rock with pines
  const rockTop = x => H * .8 - H * .1 * Math.exp(-(((x - W * .18) / (W * .3)) ** 2)) - H * .02 * N(x * .02, 40);
  for (let x = 0; x < W * .62; x++){
    const top = rockTop(x), L = H * .16 * (1 - x / (W * .62));
    if (L <= 0) continue;
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, top, 0, top + L);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(20,20,26,.92)'); g.addColorStop(.3, 'rgba(20,20,26,.5)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(20,20,26,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, top, 1.3, L);
  }
  const pine = (x, y, h, lean) => {
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(16,16,20,.9)'; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineWidth = W * .012; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x + lean * .6, y - h * .5, x + lean, y - h); ctx.stroke();
    for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++){
      const f = .3 + k * .1, bx = x + lean * f * f, by = y - h * f, side = k % 2 ? 1 : -1, span = W * (.1 - k * .009);
      for (let s = 0; s < 16; s++){
        const px = bx + side * span * (s / 16), py = by - Math.sin(s / 16 * 3.1) * H * .006;
        ctx.lineWidth = W * .004; ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(16,16,20,${(.55 + R() * .35).toFixed(2)})`;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py); ctx.lineTo(px + (R() - .5) * W * .025, py - H * (.006 + R() * .008)); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py); ctx.lineTo(px + (R() - .5) * W * .025, py + H * (.004 + R() * .006)); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  };
  pine(W * .2, rockTop(W * .2) + 2, H * .22, W * .06);
  pine(W * .34, rockTop(W * .34) + 2, H * .14, -W * .03);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,20,26,.8)'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .005;
  for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++){
    const bx = W * (.62 + b * .07), by = H * (.1 + b * .018), s = W * .022;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx - s, by - s * .2); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx - s * .4, by - s * .5, bx, by); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx + s * .4, by - s * .5, bx + s, by - s * .25); ctx.stroke();
  }
  const sz = W * .1, sxp = W * .78, syp = H * .9;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#b3302a'; ctx.fillRect(sxp, syp, sz, sz * 1.1);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(245,230,210,.85)'; ctx.lineWidth = sz * .07;
  ctx.strokeRect(sxp + sz * .16, syp + sz * .16, sz * .68, sz * .78);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sxp + sz * .5, syp + sz * .22); ctx.lineTo(sxp + sz * .5, syp + sz * .88); ctx.moveTo(sxp + sz * .24, syp + sz * .55); ctx.lineTo(sxp + sz * .76, syp + sz * .55); ctx.stroke();
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 8, R);
}

function genGeometric(ctx, W, H, R){
  const j = v => v + (R() - .5) * W * .02;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#eee2c7'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .45, H * .45, 0, W * .5, H * .5, W * .7);
  vg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,250,235,.3)'); vg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,90,50,.18)');
  ctx.fillStyle = vg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const cx = j(W * .33), cy = j(H * .47), r = H * .31;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(240,206,120,.45)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 1.3, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#1b1b1b'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .003;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * .62, H * 1.1, H * .95, Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.72); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#23407a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#e2b13a'; ctx.lineWidth = H * .032; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * .6, 0, 6.2832); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#141414'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * .2, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#c63b2b';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(j(W * .5), H * .9); ctx.lineTo(j(W * .76), H * .14); ctx.lineTo(j(W * .93), H * .82); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#d58f2e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j(W * .83), H * .3, H * .12, Math.PI * .1, Math.PI * 1.1); ctx.fill();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#151515'; ctx.lineWidth = H * .034; ctx.lineCap = 'butt';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W * .06, H * .8); ctx.lineTo(W * .7, H * .08); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.lineWidth = W * .0035;
  for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++){ ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W * (.58 + k * .018), H * .06); ctx.lineTo(W * (.98), H * (.36 + k * .045)); ctx.stroke(); }
  const q = W * .026;
  for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++){ ctx.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#eee2c7' : '#171717'; ctx.fillRect(W * .08 + k * q, H * .88, q, q); ctx.fillStyle = k % 2 ? '#171717' : '#eee2c7'; ctx.fillRect(W * .08 + k * q, H * .88 + q, q, q); }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#171717'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .002; ctx.strokeRect(W * .08, H * .88, q * 10, q * 2);
  const dots = [['#c63b2b', .56, .52, .028], ['#141414', .62, .66, .018], ['#23407a', .9, .12, .022], ['#e2b13a', .12, .16, .03]];
  for (const [c, x, y, rr] of dots){ ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(j(W * x), j(H * y), W * rr, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#141414'; ctx.lineWidth = W * .004;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(W * .66, H * .6, W * .06, 0, 6.2832); ctx.stroke();
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 14, R);
}

function genFlow(ctx, W, H, R, N){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe8da'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const C = { x: W * .55, y: H * .42, r: W * .27 };
  const outer = ['#173f4f', '#1c2733', '#2f6a73', '#0f1b24', '#24555f'];
  const inner = ['#b4502f', '#cf7440', '#8e3522', '#c05a33'];
  const sc = 2.2 / W, step = W / 320;
  const count = Math.round(1400 * (W * H) / (544 * 704));
  ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++){
    let x = R() * W, y = R() * H;
    const inside = Math.hypot(x - C.x, y - C.y) < C.r;
    const pal = inside ? inner : outer;
    ctx.strokeStyle = R() < .05 ? '#c69a3c' : pal[Math.floor(R() * pal.length)];
    ctx.globalAlpha = .5 + R() * .4;
    ctx.lineWidth = W / 700 * (.6 + R() * 1.4);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y);
    const steps = 30 + R() * 70;
    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++){
      let ang = N(x * sc, y * sc) * Math.PI * 2.6;
      const dx = x - C.x, dy = y - C.y, dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      const w = Math.exp(-(((dist - C.r) / (C.r * .45)) ** 2));
      const tang = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI / 2;
      ang = ang * (1 - w) + tang * w;
      x += Math.cos(ang) * step; y += Math.sin(ang) * step;
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x > W || y > H) break;
      ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  grainPass(ctx, W, H, 8, R);
}

function genMosaic(ctx, W, H, R){
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2a2017'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const t = W / 36, cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2, discR = W * .3;
  const tile = (x, y, s, ang, kind) => {
    let base;
    if (kind === 'gold'){ const l = 42 + R() * 30; base = [`hsl(${(40 + R() * 9).toFixed(0)},${(62 + R() * 20).toFixed(0)}%,${(l + 14).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(${(38 + R() * 8).toFixed(0)},70%,${(l - 12).toFixed(0)}%)`]; if (R() < .06) base[0] = '#fff3c4'; }
    else if (kind === 'lapis'){ const l = 26 + R() * 14; base = [`hsl(${(218 + R() * 10).toFixed(0)},62%,${(l + 10).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(222,66%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    else if (kind === 'red'){ const l = 28 + R() * 10; base = [`hsl(${(356 + R() * 8).toFixed(0)},68%,${(l + 10).toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(358,70%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    else { const l = 84 + R() * 10; base = [`hsl(44,40%,${l.toFixed(0)}%)`, `hsl(42,30%,${(l - 14).toFixed(0)}%)`]; }
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(ang + (R() - .5) * .12);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-s / 2, -s / 2, s / 2, s / 2);
    g.addColorStop(0, base[0]); g.addColorStop(1, base[1]);
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    const w = s * (.86 + R() * .1), h = s * (.84 + R() * .1);
    ctx.fillRect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h);
    ctx.restore();
  };
  for (let row = 0, y = t / 2; y < H + t; y += t, row++){
    const off = (row % 2) * t * .5;
    for (let x = t / 2 - off; x < W + t; x += t){
      if (Math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy) < discR + t * 1.6) continue;
      tile(x + (R() - .5) * t * .1, y + (R() - .5) * t * .1, t, 0, 'gold');
    }
  }
  for (let ring = 0, r = t * .55; r < discR + t * 1.6; r += t * .98, ring++){
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(6.2832 * r / t));
    const ph = R() * 6.2832;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++){
      const a = ph + k / n * 6.2832, x = cx + Math.cos(a) * r, y = cy + Math.sin(a) * r;
      let kind = 'gold';
      const rel = r / discR;
      const ray = Math.floor(((a % 6.2832) + 6.2832) % 6.2832 / (6.2832 / 16)) % 2;
      if (rel < .22) kind = ring === 0 ? 'white' : 'gold';
      else if (rel < .34) kind = 'red';
      else if (rel < .48) kind = 'gold';
      else if (rel < .8) kind = ray ? 'lapis' : 'gold';
      else if (rel < .92) kind = 'red';
      else if (rel < 1.02) kind = 'lapis';
      else kind = 'gold';
      tile(x, y, t * .98, a, kind);
    }
  }
  const sh = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * .3, H * .25, 0, W * .3, H * .25, W * .8);
  sh.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,240,200,.14)'); sh.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)');
  ctx.fillStyle = sh; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
}

/* ============================================================ geometry */
const G = { floorY: 200, ceilY: -220, half: 300, zNear: 400, zFar: -2300 };
const LEN = G.zNear - G.zFar, HGT = G.floorY - G.ceilY, MIDZ = (G.zNear + G.zFar) / 2, MIDY = (G.floorY + G.ceilY) / 2;
const RES = Math.min(4, 3 * DPR);
const real = $('#real'), mirror = $('#mirror'), world = $('#world'), stage = $('#stage');

function plane(cls, w, h, tf){
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'pl ' + cls;
  d.style.width = w + 'px'; d.style.height = h + 'px';
  d.style.marginLeft = (-w / 2) + 'px'; d.style.marginTop = (-h / 2) + 'px';
  d.style.transform = tf;
  return d;
}

/* textures (canvas → data URL, generated locally) */
function plasterTex(){
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = 128;
  const x = c.getContext('2d'), img = x.createImageData(128, 128), d = img.data, R = mulberry32(5);
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4){ const v = R() < .5 ? 0 : 255; d[i] = d[i + 1] = d[i + 2] = v; d[i + 3] = Math.round(R() * 10); }
  x.putImageData(img, 0, 0); return c.toDataURL();
}
function marbleTex(){
  const S = 256, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = S;
  const x = c.getContext('2d'), img = x.createImageData(S, S), d = img.data;
  const R = mulberry32(77), N = makeNoise(R), half = S / 2;
  for (let y = 0; y < S; y++) for (let xx = 0; xx < S; xx++){
    const tile = ((xx / half | 0) + (y / half | 0)) & 1;
    const nx = xx / S * 3.2 + tile * 13.1, ny = y / S * 3.2 + tile * 7.7;
    const n = fbm(N, nx, ny, 4);
    const v = Math.abs(Math.sin((nx * .8 + ny * 1.3 + n * 3.6) * Math.PI));
    const vein = Math.pow(1 - Math.min(1, v / .1), 2) * .85 + Math.pow(1 - Math.min(1, v / .45), 3) * .22;
    let r, g, b;
    if (tile){ r = 138 - vein * 62 + n * 14; g = 133 - vein * 62 + n * 14; b = 124 - vein * 58 + n * 12; }
    else { r = 16 + vein * 84 + n * 8; g = 15 + vein * 78 + n * 8; b = 16 + vein * 72 + n * 8; }
    if (xx % half < 1 || y % half < 1){ r *= .45; g *= .45; b *= .45; }
    const i = (y * S + xx) * 4;
    d[i] = r; d[i + 1] = g; d[i + 2] = b; d[i + 3] = 188;
  }
  x.putImageData(img, 0, 0); return c.toDataURL();
}

function wallBg(side, plaster){
  const L = [], S = [];
  for (const w of WORKS.filter(v => v.side === side)){
    const lx = side === 'L' ? (G.zNear - w.z) : (w.z - G.zFar);
    const ly = w.cy - G.ceilY;
    L.push(`radial-gradient(ellipse ${Math.round(w.w * .95 + 70)}px ${Math.round(w.h * .9 + 150)}px at ${lx}px ${ly - 34}px, rgba(255,243,220,.97) 0%, rgba(246,232,206,.72) 30%, rgba(233,226,214,.28) 58%, rgba(233,226,214,0) 76%)`);
    S.push('auto');
  }
  L.push('linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,.6) 0px, rgba(0,0,0,0) 34px)'); S.push('auto');
  L.push('linear-gradient(0deg, #17130f 0px, #17130f 13px, #4a4035 13px, #4a4035 15px, rgba(0,0,0,.45) 15px, rgba(0,0,0,0) 70px)'); S.push('auto');
  L.push(URLF(plaster)); S.push('128px 128px');
  L.push('linear-gradient(180deg, #3f372d 0%, #53493c 40%, #4a4135 100%)'); S.push('auto');
  return { img: L.join(','), size: S.join(',') };
}

  const L = [], S = [];
  for (const w of WORKS){

  }
  L.push('radial-gradient(ellipse 260px 240px at 300px 90px, rgba(255,236,200,.18), rgba(255,236,200,0) 75%)'); S.push('auto');
  L.push('linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(0,0,0,.55) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 9%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 91%, rgba(0,0,0,.55) 100%)'); S.push('auto');
  L.push(URLF(marble)); S.push('128px 128px');
  return { img: L.join(','), size: S.join(',') };
}

}

function paintingEl(w, i, refl){
  const f = w.frame, ow = w.w + 2 * (f.fw + f.mw), oh = w.h + 2 * (f.fw + f.mw);
  const x = w.side === 'L' ? -G.half + 1.5 : G.half - 1.5;
  const el = plane('frame ' + f.kind + (refl ? '' : ' paint'), ow, oh, `translate3d(${x}px, ${w.cy}px, ${w.z}px) rotateY(${w.side === 'L' ? 90 : -90}deg)`);
  el.style.padding = f.fw + 'px'; el.style.setProperty('--fw', f.fw + 'px');
  const mat = document.createElement('div'); mat.className = 'mat' + (f.mat ? ' ' + f.mat : '');
  if (f.mw) mat.style.padding = f.mw + 'px';
  const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
  mat.appendChild(cv); el.appendChild(mat);
  if (f.kind === 'gold' || w.key === 'mosaic'){ const s = document.createElement('div'); s.className = 'sheen'; (w.key === 'mosaic' ? mat : el).appendChild(s); }
  if (!refl){
    el.dataset.i = i;
    el.addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); if (dragMoved) return; approach(i); });
  }
  return { el, cv };
}
function placardEl(w){
  const pw = 66, ph = 34;
  const off = w.w / 2 + w.frame.fw + w.frame.mw + 26 + pw / 2;
  const x = w.side === 'L' ? -G.half + 1.2 : G.half - 1.2;
  const z = w.side === 'L' ? w.z - off : w.z + off;
  const el = plane('placard', pw * 4, ph * 4, `translate3d(${x}px, ${w.cy + w.h / 2 - 12}px, ${z}px) rotateY(${w.side === 'L' ? 90 : -90}deg) scale(.25)`);
  el.innerHTML = '<b></b><span></span><em></em>';
  el.children[0].textContent = w.title;
  el.children[1].textContent = w.artist + ', ' + w.year;
  el.children[2].textContent = w.medium;
  return el;
}
function beamEl(w){
  const bw = 150, x = w.side === 'L' ? -G.half + bw / 2 : G.half - bw / 2;
  const el = plane('beam-pl', bw, HGT, `translate3d(${x}px, ${MIDY}px, ${w.z}px)` + (w.side === 'R' ? ' scaleX(-1)' : ''));
  const top = w.cy - w.h / 2 - G.ceilY - 10, bot = w.cy + w.h / 2 - G.ceilY + 30;
  el.style.clipPath = `polygon(124px 0, 134px 0, 0 ${bot}px, 0 ${top}px)`;
  el.style.background = 'linear-gradient(200deg, rgba(255,238,205,.15) 0%, rgba(255,238,205,.05) 55%, rgba(255,238,205,.02) 100%)';
  return el;
}

function buildScene(){
  const plaster = plasterTex(), marble = marbleTex();
  const mk = (grp, refl) => {
    const wl = wallBg('L', plaster), wr = wallBg('R', plaster);
    const L = plane('wall-pl', LEN, HGT, `translate3d(${-G.half}px, ${MIDY}px, ${MIDZ}px) rotateY(90deg)`);
    L.style.backgroundImage = wl.img; L.style.backgroundSize = wl.size;
    const Rw = plane('wall-pl', LEN, HGT, `translate3d(${G.half}px, ${MIDY}px, ${MIDZ}px) rotateY(-90deg)`);
    Rw.style.backgroundImage = wr.img; Rw.style.backgroundSize = wr.size;
    const E = plane('end-pl', G.half * 2, HGT, `translate3d(0px, ${MIDY}px, ${G.zFar}px)`);
    E.style.background = 'radial-gradient(ellipse 280px 330px at 300px 150px, rgba(255,243,220,.82), rgba(240,228,206,.45) 42%, rgba(233,226,214,0) 78%), linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,.55), rgba(0,0,0,0) 34px), linear-gradient(0deg, #17130f 0 13px, #4a4035 13px 15px, rgba(0,0,0,0) 15px), linear-gradient(180deg, #3f372d, #4f463a)';
    grp.append(L, Rw, E);
    if (!refl){
      const t = plane('end-title', 2200, 760, `translate3d(0px, -58px, ${G.zFar + 1}px) scale(.25)`);
      t.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      t.innerHTML = '<small>Salle des Huit</small><b>Galerie Nocturne</b><i></i><span>eight works · after hours</span>';
      grp.appendChild(t);
      E.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!dragMoved) walkTo(G.zFar + 260); });
      L.addEventListener('click', e => wallClick('L', e));
      Rw.addEventListener('click', e => wallClick('R', e));
      const fb = floorBg(marble), cb = ceilBg();
      const F = plane('floor-pl', G.half * 2, LEN, `translate3d(0px, ${G.floorY}px, ${MIDZ}px) rotateX(90deg)`);
      F.style.backgroundImage = fb.img; F.style.backgroundSize = fb.size;
      F.addEventListener('click', e => { if (dragMoved) return; const z = e.offsetY + G.zFar; walkTo(z + 120); });
      const C = plane('ceil-pl', G.half * 2, LEN, `translate3d(0px, ${G.ceilY}px, ${MIDZ}px) rotateX(-90deg)`);
      C.style.backgroundImage = cb.img; C.style.backgroundSize = cb.size;
      grp.append(F, C);
    }
    WORKS.forEach((w, i) => {
      const p = paintingEl(w, i, refl);
      grp.appendChild(p.el);
      if (refl) w.cvRefl = p.cv; else { w.cv = p.cv; w.el = p.el; grp.appendChild(placardEl(w)); grp.appendChild(beamEl(w)); }
    });
  };
  mk(mirror, true);
  mk(real, false);
}
function wallClick(side, e){
  if (dragMoved) return;
  const z = side === 'L' ? G.zNear - e.offsetX : e.offsetX + G.zFar;
  let best = -1, bd = 1e9;
  WORKS.forEach((w, i) => { if (w.side === side){ const d = Math.abs(w.z - z); if (d < bd){ bd = d; best = i; } } });
  if (best >= 0 && bd < 320) approach(best); else walkTo(z + 200);
}

/* ============================================================ rendering the works */
function renderWork(w){
  const c = w.cv;
  c.width = Math.round(w.w * RES); c.height = Math.round(w.h * RES);
  const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
  const R = mulberry32(w.seed), N = makeNoise(mulberry32(w.seed * 7 + 3));
  w.gen(ctx, c.width, c.height, R, N);
  c.classList.add('ready');
  const r = w.cvRefl;
  r.width = Math.round(w.w * 1.2); r.height = Math.round(w.h * 1.2);
  r.getContext('2d').drawImage(c, 0, 0, r.width, r.height);
  r.classList.add('ready');
  w.ready = true;
  if (wallBuilt) drawWallItem(w);
}

/* ============================================================ camera */
const cam = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 160, rot: 0 };
const tgt = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 160, rot: 0 };
const look = { x: 0, y: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
const input = { fwd: 0, turn: 0 };
let mode = 'corridor', current = -1, persp = 820, bob = 0, lastT = performance.now(), cardTimer = 0, idleT = 0;
const Z_MIN = G.zFar + 230, Z_MAX = 200;
function setPersp(){
  persp = Math.round(Math.max(560, Math.min(1000, window.innerWidth * .6)));
  stage.style.setProperty('--persp', persp + 'px');
}
function walkTo(z){
  if (mode === 'detail') stepBack(true);
  tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, Math.min(Z_MAX, z)); tgt.x = 0; tgt.y = 0;
  if (Math.abs(tgt.rot) > 30) tgt.rot = 0;
}
function frameFor(i){
  const w = WORKS[i];
  const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
  const oh = w.h + 2 * (w.frame.fw + w.frame.mw), ow = w.w + 2 * (w.frame.fw + w.frame.mw);
  /* frame the painting plus its placard inside the space left of the detail card */
  const cardW = Math.min(372, vw * .34) + Math.min(64, Math.max(20, vw * .04));
  const left = 48, right = vw - cardW - 56, aw = Math.max(200, right - left);
  const compW = ow + 26 + 66, compOff = (26 + 66) / 2;
  const s = Math.min((vh * .7) / oh, (aw * .94) / compW);
  const d = persp / s;
  const cxs = (left + right) / 2;
  const shift = (vw / 2 - cxs) / s + compOff;
  const sign = w.side === 'L' ? 1 : -1;
  return { x: w.side === 'L' ? -G.half + d : G.half - d, y: w.cy, z: w.z - sign * shift, rot: w.side === 'L' ? -90 : 90 };
}
function approach(i){
  if (i < 0 || i >= WORKS.length) return;
  mode = 'detail'; current = i;
  Object.assign(tgt, frameFor(i));
  document.body.classList.add('detail');
  hideCard();
  clearTimeout(cardTimer);
  fillCard(i);
  updateIdx();
  if (phoneMQ.matches) showCard();
}
function stepBack(silent){
  if (mode !== 'detail') return;
  mode = 'corridor';
  const w = WORKS[current];
  tgt.x = 0; tgt.y = 0; tgt.rot = 0;
  tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, Math.min(Z_MAX, w.z + 260));
  document.body.classList.remove('detail');
  hideCard();
  if (!silent) stage.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}
function stepWork(dir){
  const n = current < 0 ? (dir > 0 ? 0 : WORKS.length - 1) : (current + dir + WORKS.length) % WORKS.length;
  approach(n);
}
function nearest(){
  let best = 0, bd = 1e9;
  const side = cam.rot < -25 ? 'L' : cam.rot > 25 ? 'R' : null;
  WORKS.forEach((w, i) => {
    if (side && w.side !== side) return;
    const ahead = cam.z - w.z;
    const d = Math.abs(ahead - (side ? 0 : 220)) + (ahead < -60 && !side ? 600 : 0);
    if (d < bd){ bd = d; best = i; }
  });
  return best;
}

/* ============================================================ card + HUD */
const card = $('#card');
function fillCard(i){
  const w = WORKS[i];
  $('#cNo').textContent = `No. ${ROMAN[i]} / VIII`;
  $('#cTitle').textContent = w.title;
  $('#cArtist').innerHTML = '';
  const a = document.createElement('span'); a.textContent = ' (' + w.life + ', fictional)';
  $('#cArtist').append(w.artist, a);
  $('#cYear').textContent = w.year; $('#cMedium').textContent = w.medium; $('#cDims').textContent = w.dims;
  $('#cNote').textContent = w.note;
  $('#cSeed').textContent = `Generated live in your browser · seed ${w.seed}`;
  const ci = $('#cImg');
  if (w.ready){
    const mw = Math.min(window.innerWidth - 40, 520);
    const s = Math.min(mw / w.w, (window.innerHeight * .34) / w.h);
    ci.width = Math.round(w.w * s * DPR); ci.height = Math.round(w.h * s * DPR);
    ci.style.width = Math.round(w.w * s) + 'px'; ci.style.height = Math.round(w.h * s) + 'px';
    ci.getContext('2d').drawImage(w.cv, 0, 0, ci.width, ci.height);
  }
}
function showCard(){ card.classList.add('show'); card.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false'); }
function hideCard(){ card.classList.remove('show'); card.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true'); }
$('#cPrev').addEventListener('click', () => stepWork(-1));
$('#cNext').addEventListener('click', () => stepWork(1));
$('#cBack').addEventListener('click', () => { if (phoneMQ.matches){ mode = 'corridor'; current = -1; document.body.classList.remove('detail'); hideCard(); } else stepBack(); });
$('#nGo').addEventListener('click', () => approach(nearest()));

const marks = $('#planMarks'), planCam = $('#planCam'), idx = $('#idx');
const SVGNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
const MARK_COL = ['#e0894e', '#cf2a1f', '#c24a33', '#e8e4dc', '#7b7f86', '#23407a', '#2f6a73', '#d6b056'];
WORKS.forEach((w, i) => {
  const r = document.createElementNS(SVGNS, 'rect');
  const mx = G.zNear - w.z - w.w / 2;
  r.setAttribute('x', mx); r.setAttribute('width', w.w);
  r.setAttribute('y', w.side === 'L' ? -38 : 600 + 8); r.setAttribute('height', 30); r.setAttribute('rx', 6);
  r.setAttribute('fill', MARK_COL[i]); r.setAttribute('class', 'mk');
  r.addEventListener('click', () => approach(i));
  marks.appendChild(r);
  const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = ROMAN[i];
  b.setAttribute('aria-label', `Go to No. ${ROMAN[i]}, ${w.title}`);
  b.addEventListener('click', () => approach(i));
  idx.appendChild(b);
});
function updateIdx(){ [...idx.children].forEach((b, i) => b.setAttribute('aria-current', String(mode === 'detail' && i === current))); }
let lastNear = -2;
function updateHUD(){
  planCam.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${(G.zNear - cam.z).toFixed(1)} ${(cam.x + G.half).toFixed(1)}) rotate(${cam.rot.toFixed(1)})`);
  const pz = G.zNear - cam.z;
  $('#planPos').textContent = mode === 'detail' ? 'No. ' + ROMAN[current] : pz < 380 ? 'Entrance' : pz > 2350 ? 'End wall' : Math.round(pz / 100) / 10 * 3 + ' m';
  if (mode === 'corridor'){
    const n = nearest();
    if (n !== lastNear){
      lastNear = n; const w = WORKS[n];
      $('#nNo').textContent = `No. ${ROMAN[n]} · ${w.side === 'L' ? 'left wall' : 'right wall'}`;
      $('#nTitle').textContent = w.title;
      $('#nMeta').textContent = `${w.artist}, ${w.year}`;
    }
  }
}

/* ============================================================ frame loop */
function tick(t){
  const dt = Math.min(.05, (t - lastT) / 1000); lastT = t;
  if (!document.hidden && !phoneMQ.matches){
    if (mode === 'corridor'){
      if (input.fwd) tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, Math.min(Z_MAX, tgt.z - input.fwd * 520 * dt));
      if (input.turn) tgt.rot = Math.max(-90, Math.min(90, tgt.rot + input.turn * 110 * dt));
    }
    const k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * (reduced.matches ? 18 : 3.4));
    const vmax = (reduced.matches ? 6000 : 1300) * dt, rmax = (reduced.matches ? 900 : 150) * dt;
    const step = (a, b, lim) => { let s = (b - a) * k; if (s > lim) s = lim; if (s < -lim) s = -lim; return a + s; };
    const oz = cam.z;
    cam.x = step(cam.x, tgt.x, vmax); cam.y = step(cam.y, tgt.y, vmax); cam.z = step(cam.z, tgt.z, vmax); cam.rot = step(cam.rot, tgt.rot, rmax);
    const moved = Math.abs(cam.z - oz);
    if (!reduced.matches) bob += moved * .045;
    idleT += dt;
    look.x += (look.tx - look.x) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt * 4)); look.y += (look.ty - look.y) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt * 4));
    const sway = reduced.matches ? 0 : Math.sin(idleT * .35) * .5;
    const lx = mode === 'corridor' ? look.x * 7 + sway : look.x * 2;
    const ly = mode === 'corridor' ? look.y * 4 : look.y * 1.2;
    const by = Math.sin(bob) * 1.6;
    world.style.transform = `translateZ(${persp}px) rotateX(${ly.toFixed(3)}deg) rotateY(${(cam.rot + lx).toFixed(3)}deg) translate3d(${(-cam.x).toFixed(2)}px, ${(-cam.y - by).toFixed(2)}px, ${(-cam.z).toFixed(2)}px)`;
    const endDist = cam.z - G.zFar;
    const fog = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (endDist - 500) / 1300)) * Math.max(0, 1 - Math.abs(cam.rot) / 55);
    stage.style.setProperty('--fog', fog.toFixed(3));
    world.style.setProperty('--sheen', (50 + ((cam.z * .08 + cam.x * .1 + cam.rot * .4) % 60)).toFixed(1));
    updateHUD();
    if (mode === 'detail'){
      const settled = Math.abs(cam.x - tgt.x) < 6 && Math.abs(cam.z - tgt.z) < 6 && Math.abs(cam.rot - tgt.rot) < 2;
      if (settled && !card.classList.contains('show')) showCard();
    }
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

/* ============================================================ input */
const keys = new Set();
function syncKeys(){
  input.fwd = (keys.has('f') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('b') ? 1 : 0);
  input.turn = (keys.has('r') ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has('l') ? 1 : 0);
}
const KEYMAP = { arrowup: 'f', w: 'f', arrowdown: 'b', s: 'b', arrowleft: 'l', a: 'l', arrowright: 'r', d: 'r' };
window.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return;
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  const tag = (e.target && e.target.tagName) || '';
  if (phoneMQ.matches){
    if (k === 'escape' && card.classList.contains('show')){ hideCard(); document.body.classList.remove('detail'); mode = 'corridor'; }
    else if (k === 'arrowright') railStep(1); else if (k === 'arrowleft') railStep(-1);
    return;
  }
  if (k === 'escape'){ e.preventDefault(); stepBack(); return; }
  if (/^[1-8]$/.test(k)){ approach(+k - 1); return; }
  if (mode === 'detail'){
    if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a'){ e.preventDefault(); stepWork(-1); }
    else if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd'){ e.preventDefault(); stepWork(1); }
    else if (KEYMAP[k] === 'f' || KEYMAP[k] === 'b' || k === 'backspace'){ e.preventDefault(); stepBack(); }
    return;
  }
  if (KEYMAP[k]){ if (tag === 'BUTTON' && (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'arrowright') && e.target.closest('.idx')) return; e.preventDefault(); keys.add(KEYMAP[k]); syncKeys(); }
  else if ((k === 'enter' || k === ' ') && tag !== 'BUTTON'){ e.preventDefault(); approach(nearest()); }
  else if (k === 'home'){ walkTo(Z_MAX - 40); }
});
window.addEventListener('keyup', e => { const k = KEYMAP[e.key.toLowerCase()]; if (k){ keys.delete(k); syncKeys(); } });
window.addEventListener('blur', () => { keys.clear(); syncKeys(); });

document.querySelectorAll('.pbtn').forEach(b => {
  const act = b.dataset.act;
  const map = { fwd: 'f', back: 'b', left: 'l', right: 'r' };
  if (act === 'approach'){ b.addEventListener('click', () => approach(nearest())); return; }
  b.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
    e.preventDefault(); b.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); b.classList.add('held');
    if (mode === 'detail') stepBack(true);
    keys.add(map[act]); syncKeys();
  });
  const up = () => { b.classList.remove('held'); keys.delete(map[act]); syncKeys(); };
  b.addEventListener('pointerup', up); b.addEventListener('pointercancel', up); b.addEventListener('lostpointercapture', up);
  b.addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (e.detail !== 0) return;
    if (mode === 'detail') stepBack(true);
    if (act === 'fwd') tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, tgt.z - 240);
    else if (act === 'back') tgt.z = Math.min(Z_MAX, tgt.z + 240);
    else if (act === 'left') tgt.rot = Math.max(-90, tgt.rot - 45);
    else if (act === 'right') tgt.rot = Math.min(90, tgt.rot + 45);
  });
});

let drag = null, dragMoved = false;
stage.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  if (e.button !== 0) return;
  drag = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, z: tgt.z, rot: tgt.rot, id: e.pointerId };
  dragMoved = false;
});
window.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
  look.tx = (e.clientX / vw - .5) * 2; look.ty = (.5 - e.clientY / vh) * 2;
  if (!drag || drag.id !== e.pointerId) return;
  const dx = e.clientX - drag.x, dy = e.clientY - drag.y;
  if (!dragMoved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 7){ dragMoved = true; stage.classList.add('dragging'); if (mode === 'detail') stepBack(true); }
  if (dragMoved){
    tgt.rot = Math.max(-90, Math.min(90, drag.rot - dx * .18));
    tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, Math.min(Z_MAX, drag.z + dy * 2.2));
  }
});
const endDrag = () => { if (drag){ drag = null; stage.classList.remove('dragging'); setTimeout(() => { dragMoved = false; }, 0); } };
window.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag); window.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);
stage.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  if (mode === 'detail'){ if (e.deltaY < -20) stepBack(true); return; }
  tgt.z = Math.max(Z_MIN, Math.min(Z_MAX, tgt.z - e.deltaY * .9));
}, { passive: false });
stage.addEventListener('scroll', () => { stage.scrollTop = 0; stage.scrollLeft = 0; });
document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { look.tx = 0; look.ty = 0; });

/* ============================================================ phone wall */
const rail = $('#rail'), dots = $('#dots');
let wallBuilt = false;
function buildWall(){
  if (wallBuilt) return;
  wallBuilt = true;
  WORKS.forEach((w, i) => {
    const item = document.createElement('figure'); item.className = 'w-item'; item.style.margin = '0';
    const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.type = 'button'; btn.className = 'w-art';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label', `No. ${ROMAN[i]}: ${w.title} by ${w.artist}. Open details`);
    const f = w.frame, s = 1;
    const fr = document.createElement('div'); fr.className = 'frame ' + f.kind;
    fr.style.setProperty('--fw', Math.max(4, Math.round(f.fw * .8)) + 'px');
    fr.style.padding = Math.max(4, Math.round(f.fw * .8)) + 'px';
    const mat = document.createElement('div'); mat.className = 'mat' + (f.mat ? ' ' + f.mat : '');
    if (f.mw) mat.style.padding = Math.round(f.mw * .9) + 'px';
    const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
    mat.appendChild(cv); fr.appendChild(mat);
    if (f.kind === 'gold'){ const sh = document.createElement('div'); sh.className = 'sheen'; fr.appendChild(sh); }
    btn.appendChild(fr);
    const cap = document.createElement('figcaption'); cap.className = 'w-cap';
    const b = document.createElement('b'); b.textContent = w.title;
    const sp = document.createElement('span'); sp.textContent = `${w.artist}, ${w.year} · ${w.medium}`;
    cap.append(b, sp);
    item.append(btn, cap);
    rail.appendChild(item);
    w.wallCv = cv; w.wallFrame = fr; w.wallMat = mat;
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => { current = i; fillCard(i); showCard(); document.body.classList.add('detail'); });
    const dot = document.createElement('i'); dots.appendChild(dot);
    void s;
  });
  sizeWall();
}
function sizeWall(){
  if (!wallBuilt) return;
  const vw = window.innerWidth, vh = window.innerHeight;
  WORKS.forEach(w => {
    const f = w.frame, fw = Math.max(4, Math.round(f.fw * .8)), mw = f.mw ? Math.round(f.mw * .9) : 0;
    const maxW = vw * .7 - 2 * (fw + mw), maxH = vh * .44 - 2 * (fw + mw);
    const s = Math.min(maxW / w.w, maxH / w.h);
    const cw = Math.round(w.w * s), ch = Math.round(w.h * s);
    w.wallCv.style.width = cw + 'px'; w.wallCv.style.height = ch + 'px';
    w.wallCv.width = Math.round(cw * DPR); w.wallCv.height = Math.round(ch * DPR);
    w.wallMat.style.width = (cw + 2 * mw) + 'px'; w.wallMat.style.height = (ch + 2 * mw) + 'px';
    w.wallFrame.style.width = (cw + 2 * (mw + fw)) + 'px'; w.wallFrame.style.height = (ch + 2 * (mw + fw)) + 'px';
    if (w.ready) drawWallItem(w);
  });
  onRail();
}
function drawWallItem(w){
  if (!w.wallCv || !w.ready) return;
  const c = w.wallCv; c.getContext('2d').drawImage(w.cv, 0, 0, c.width, c.height); c.classList.add('ready');
}
function railIndex(){ const iw = rail.children[0] ? rail.children[0].offsetWidth : 1; return Math.max(0, Math.min(WORKS.length - 1, Math.round(rail.scrollLeft / iw))); }
function onRail(){ const i = railIndex(); [...dots.children].forEach((d, k) => d.classList.toggle('on', k === i)); }
function railStep(dir){
  const iw = rail.children[0] ? rail.children[0].offsetWidth : 0;
  rail.scrollTo({ left: (railIndex() + dir) * iw, behavior: reduced.matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
}
rail.addEventListener('scroll', onRail, { passive: true });
$('#wPrev').addEventListener('click', () => railStep(-1));
$('#wNext').addEventListener('click', () => railStep(1));

/* ============================================================ modes + boot */
function applyMode(){
  const phone = phoneMQ.matches;
  document.body.classList.toggle('mode-wall', phone);
  hideCard(); document.body.classList.remove('detail');
  if (phone){ buildWall(); sizeWall(); mode = 'corridor'; current = -1; Object.assign(tgt, { x: 0, y: 0, rot: 0 }); }
  setPersp();
}
let rz = 0;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
  clearTimeout(rz);
  applyModeIfChanged();
  rz = setTimeout(() => { setPersp(); if (phoneMQ.matches) sizeWall(); if (mode === 'detail') Object.assign(tgt, frameFor(current)); }, 80);
});
let lastPhone = null;
function applyModeIfChanged(){ if (phoneMQ.matches !== lastPhone){ lastPhone = phoneMQ.matches; applyMode(); } }

buildScene();
applyModeIfChanged();
setPersp();
requestAnimationFrame(t => { lastT = t; tick(t); });

const order = WORKS.map((w, i) => i);
let done = 0;
const veil = $('#veil'), bar = $('#veilBar');
function next(){
  if (!order.length){ veil.classList.add('gone'); return; }
  const w = WORKS[order.shift()];
  try { renderWork(w); } catch (err) { w.ready = false; }
  done++; bar.style.setProperty('--p', (done / WORKS.length).toFixed(2));
  if (done === 3) veil.classList.add('gone');
  setTimeout(next, 16);
}
setTimeout(next, 30);
})();
</script>
</body>

## ============================================================================
## 039 Galerie Nocturne — 1:1 Native Nim/WASM Port of 039-virtual-gallery.html
## ============================================================================
## Architecture Overview:
##   1. Stage & Corridor Geometry: Fixed 1280x800 viewport with CSS-equivalent
##      perspective (768px) and a 600x420x2700 3D corridor box.
##   2. Deterministic PRNG & Perlin Noise: Bit-exact Mulberry32 PRNG and
##      2D gradient Perlin / 4-octave FBM noise driving 8 generative artworks
##      and procedural marble/plaster textures.
##   3. Eight Generative Artworks:
##      - I.   Evening at Port Salin (Pointillist harbour dusk, seed 1889)
##      - II.  Composition for a Quiet Street (Primary-colour grid, seed 1934)
##      - III. Ember Over Plum (Layered Rothko-style colour field, seed 1961)
##      - IV.  Swell (2x2 supersampled lens-warped Op-art checker, seed 1966)
##      - V.   Mountains After Rain (5-ridge sumi-e ink wash scroll, seed 1998)
##      - VI.  Counterpoint No. 7 (Multiply-blended Bauhaus fugue, seed 1928)
##      - VII. Currents, Interleaved (1,400-streamline flow field, seed 2019)
##      - VIII.Tessellated Sun (Concentric gold/lapis/red mosaic, seed 2011)
##   4. Software 3D Rasteriser: Per-pixel ray-cast corridor with real mirrored
##      floor reflections, perspective-correct area-averaged sprite quads,
##      volumetric spotlight cones, and dynamic camera-relative depth fog.
##   5. Interactive Museum HUD: Top-left brand header, top-right nearest-work
##      indicator with clickable "Approach" pill, bottom-center walk/view pad,
##      bottom-left live floor-plan minimap with view cone & I–VIII buttons,
##      bottom-right controls hint, and animated curatorial detail card.
## ============================================================================

import yantra
import std/[math, strutils]

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────── 1. Constants & Geometry
const
  G_FLOORY   = 200.0'f32                         ## Floor plane Y (+Y is down)
  G_CEILY    = -220.0'f32                        ## Ceiling plane Y (-Y is up)
  G_HALF     = 300.0'f32                         ## Half-width of 600px corridor
  G_ZNEAR    = 400.0'f32                         ## Near entrance wall Z
  G_ZFAR     = -2300.0'f32                       ## Far end-wall Z
  MIDZ       = (G_ZNEAR + G_ZFAR) * 0.5'f32      ## -950.0
  MIDY       = (G_FLOORY + G_CEILY) * 0.5'f32    ## -10.0

  RES        = 3                                 ## Artwork supersample factor
  SPRITE_SCALE = 1                               ## 1 texel per CSS px on frames

  SW         = 1280.0'f32                        ## Viewport width
  SH         = 800.0'f32                         ## Viewport height
  PERSP      = 768.0'f32                         ## clamp(1280 * 0.6, 560, 1000)

  RW         = 640                               ## Corridor raster width
  RH         = 400                               ## Corridor raster height
  SCALE      = SW / float32(RW)                  ## 2.0 CSS px per raster px

  Z_MIN      = G_ZFAR + 230.0'f32                ## -2070.0 walk limit
  Z_MAX      = 200.0'f32                         ## +200.0 walk limit
  STRAFE_X   = G_HALF - 60.0'f32                 ## 240.0 strafe limit
  PITCH_MAX  = 25.0'f32                          ## Drag-look pitch clamp (deg)

  TW_L       = 2700                              ## Side-wall texture width
  TH_L       = 420                               ## Side-wall texture height
  TW_F       = 600                               ## Floor/ceiling texture width
  TH_F       = 2700                              ## Floor/ceiling texture length
  TW_E       = 600                               ## End-wall texture width
  TH_E       = 420                               ## End-wall texture height

  PLAC_W     = 66                                ## Placard world width
  PLAC_H     = 34                                ## Placard world height
  TITLE_W    = 550                               ## End-wall title world width
  TITLE_H    = 190                               ## End-wall title world height

  BRAND_L    = 38.4'f32
  BRAND_T    = 24.0'f32
  NEAR_R     = 38.4'f32
  NEAR_T     = 25.6'f32
  PAD_B      = 27.2'f32
  PLAN_L     = 38.4'f32
  PLAN_B     = 27.2'f32
  HINT_R     = 38.4'f32
  HINT_B     = 27.2'f32
  CARD_R     = 51.2'f32
  CARD_W     = 372.0'f32
  PLAN_W     = 240.0'f32
  PLAN_SVG_H = 240.0'f32 * (780.0'f32 / 2820.0'f32)

  TXT_BL     = 1.0833'f32                        ## SkParagraph baseline offset

  C_PAPER    = 0xFFF1E9DA'u32
  C_GOLD     = 0xFFC9A45C'u32
  C_GOLDHI   = 0xFFECD49A'u32
  C_MUTED    = 0xFFA89A80'u32
  C_KICK     = 0xFFBFAE8C'u32
  C_NIGHT    = 0xFF0B0A09'u32

  ROMAN: array[8, string] = ["I", "II", "III", "IV", "V", "VI", "VII", "VIII"]
  MARK_COL: array[8, uint32] = [
    0xFFE0894E'u32, 0xFFCF2A1F'u32, 0xFFC24A33'u32, 0xFFE8E4DC'u32,
    0xFF7B7F86'u32, 0xFF23407A'u32, 0xFF2F6A73'u32, 0xFFD6B056'u32
  ]

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────── 2. Colour & Math Helpers
proc cl(f, lo, hi: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  if f < lo: lo elif f > hi: hi else: f

proc rnd32(x: float32): int32 {.inline.} =
  ## Pure integer rounding (avoids `env.round` WASM import trap).
  int32(if x < 0.0'f32: x - 0.5'f32 else: x + 0.5'f32)

proc clampByte(v: int32): int32 {.inline.} =
  if v < 0: 0 elif v > 255: 255 else: v

proc rgba(r, g, b: int32; a: float32): uint32 {.inline.} =
  (uint32(clampByte(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32))) shl 24) or
  (uint32(clampByte(r)) shl 16) or
  (uint32(clampByte(g)) shl 8) or
  uint32(clampByte(b))

proc hex(h: int32): uint32 {.inline.} =
  0xFF000000'u32 or (uint32(h) and 0xFFFFFF'u32)

proc rgbOf(c: uint32): array[3, int32] {.inline.} =
  [int32((c shr 16) and 0xFF'u32), int32((c shr 8) and 0xFF'u32), int32(c and 0xFF'u32)]

proc alphaOf(c: uint32): float32 {.inline.} =
  float32((c shr 24) and 0xFF'u32) / 255.0'f32

proc withA(c: uint32; a: float32): uint32 {.inline.} =
  (c and 0x00FFFFFF'u32) or (uint32(clampByte(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32))) shl 24)

proc mix(c0, c1: uint32; t: float32): uint32 {.inline.} =
  let u = cl(t, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  let a = rgbOf(c0)
  let b = rgbOf(c1)
  rgba(
    a[0] + int32(float32(b[0] - a[0]) * u),
    a[1] + int32(float32(b[1] - a[1]) * u),
    a[2] + int32(float32(b[2] - a[2]) * u),
    alphaOf(c0) + (alphaOf(c1) - alphaOf(c0)) * u
  )

proc fmod32(x, m: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  ## Pure float32 modulo (avoids `fmodf` link dependency in standalone minilibc).
  x - trunc(x / m) * m

proc hsl(h, s, l: float32): uint32 =
  var hh = fmod32(h, 360.0'f32)
  if hh < 0.0'f32: hh += 360.0'f32
  let c = (1.0'f32 - abs(2.0'f32 * l - 1.0'f32)) * s
  let hp = hh / 60.0'f32
  let x = c * (1.0'f32 - abs(fmod32(hp, 2.0'f32) - 1.0'f32))
  let m = l - c * 0.5'f32
  var r, g, b: float32
  if hp < 1.0'f32:   (r, g, b) = (c, x, 0.0'f32)
  elif hp < 2.0'f32: (r, g, b) = (x, c, 0.0'f32)
  elif hp < 3.0'f32: (r, g, b) = (0.0'f32, c, x)
  elif hp < 4.0'f32: (r, g, b) = (0.0'f32, x, c)
  elif hp < 5.0'f32: (r, g, b) = (x, 0.0'f32, c)
  else:              (r, g, b) = (c, 0.0'f32, x)
  rgba(
    int32((r + m) * 255.0'f32 + 0.5'f32),
    int32((g + m) * 255.0'f32 + 0.5'f32),
    int32((b + m) * 255.0'f32 + 0.5'f32),
    1.0'f32
  )

proc lerpF(a, b, t: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  a + (b - a) * t

proc cubicBezier(x1, y1, x2, y2, x: float32): float32 =
  if x <= 0.0'f32: return 0.0'f32
  if x >= 1.0'f32: return 1.0'f32
  var lo = 0.0'f32
  var hi = 1.0'f32
  for _ in 0 .. 18:
    let m = (lo + hi) * 0.5'f32
    let inv = 1.0'f32 - m
    let bx = 3.0'f32 * inv * inv * m * x1 + 3.0'f32 * inv * m * m * x2 + m * m * m
    if bx < x: lo = m else: hi = m
  let m = (lo + hi) * 0.5'f32
  let inv = 1.0'f32 - m
  3.0'f32 * inv * inv * m * y1 + 3.0'f32 * inv * m * m * y2 + m * m * m

proc cssEase(t: float32): float32 =
  cubicBezier(0.25'f32, 0.1'f32, 0.25'f32, 1.0'f32, t)

# ─────────────────────────────── 3. Deterministic PRNG & Perlin FBM Noise
type
  Rng = ref object
    a: uint32

  Noise = object
    perm: array[512, uint8]

proc rngInit(seed: int32): Rng =
  new(result)
  result.a = uint32(seed) and 0x7FFFFFFF'u32

proc nextF(r: Rng): float32 =
  r.a = r.a + 0x6D2B79F5'u32
  var t = r.a
  t = (t xor (t shr 15)) * (1'u32 or t)
  t = t + ((t xor (t shr 7)) * (61'u32 or t)) xor t
  float32((t xor (t shr 14)) shr 0) / 4294967296.0'f32

proc makeNoise(seed: int32): Noise =
  var r = rngInit(seed)
  var base: array[256, uint8]
  for i in 0 .. 255: base[i] = uint8(i)
  for i in countdown(255, 1):
    let j = int32(r.nextF() * float32(i + 1))
    let t = base[i]; base[i] = base[j]; base[j] = t
  for i in 0 .. 511: result.perm[i] = base[i and 255]

proc grad(h: uint8; x, y: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  case h and 7'u8
  of 0: x + y
  of 1: -x + y
  of 2: x - y
  of 3: -x - y
  of 4: x
  of 5: -x
  of 6: y
  else: -y

proc fade(t: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  t * t * t * (t * (t * 6.0'f32 - 15.0'f32) + 10.0'f32)

proc noiseAt(n: Noise; x, y: float32): float32 =
  let xf = floor(x)
  let yf = floor(y)
  let X = int(int32(xf) and 255)
  let Y = int(int32(yf) and 255)
  let fx = x - xf
  let fy = y - yf
  let u = fade(fx)
  let v = fade(fy)
  let aa = n.perm[int(n.perm[X]) + Y]
  let ab = n.perm[int(n.perm[X]) + Y + 1]
  let ba = n.perm[int(n.perm[X + 1]) + Y]
  let bb = n.perm[int(n.perm[X + 1]) + Y + 1]
  let g1 = grad(aa, fx, fy)
  let g2 = grad(ba, fx - 1.0'f32, fy)
  let g3 = grad(ab, fx, fy - 1.0'f32)
  let g4 = grad(bb, fx - 1.0'f32, fy - 1.0'f32)
  let l1 = g1 + u * (g2 - g1)
  let l2 = g3 + u * (g4 - g3)
  (l1 + v * (l2 - l1)) * 0.7'f32

proc fbm(n: Noise; x, y: float32; oct: int32): float32 =
  var s = 0.0'f32
  var a = 0.5'f32
  var f = 1.0'f32
  for _ in 0 ..< oct:
    s += a * n.noiseAt(x * f, y * f)
    f *= 2.03'f32
    a *= 0.5'f32
  s

# ──────────────────────────────────────── 4. Collection Metadata (8 Works)

    side: char

    gold: bool
    fw, mw: float32
    matWhite: bool
    seed: int32
    gen: GenKind
    title, artist, life, year, medium, dims, note: string

const WORKS: array[8, Work] = [

proc frameOuter(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = w.fw + w.mw
proc outerW(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = w.w + 2.0'f32 * w.frameOuter
proc outerH(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = w.h + 2.0'f32 * w.frameOuter
proc paintX(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = (if w.side == 'L': -G_HALF + 1.5'f32 else: G_HALF - 1.5'f32)
proc placX(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = (if w.side == 'L': -G_HALF + 1.2'f32 else: G_HALF - 1.2'f32)
proc placZ(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} =
  let off = w.w * 0.5'f32 + w.fw + w.mw + 59.0'f32
  if w.side == 'L': w.z - off else: w.z + off
proc placY(w: Work): float32 {.inline.} = w.cy + w.h * 0.5'f32 - 12.0'f32

# ──────────────────────────── 5. Custom Draw Primitives & Gradient Helpers
type
  Stop = tuple[pos: float32, col: uint32]

proc dnum(x: float32): string =
  let v = int(abs(x) * 100.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)
  (if x < 0.0'f32 and v > 0: "-" else: "") & $(v div 100) & "." & align($(v mod 100), 2, '0')

proc ell(d: Draw; cx, cy, rx, ry: float32; argb: uint32): Draw =
  result = d
  result.cmds.add "E " & dnum(cx) & " " & dnum(cy) & " " & dnum(rx) & " " &
                 dnum(ry) & " " & $argb & ";"

proc rectStroke(d: Draw; x, y, w, h, radius: float32; argb: uint32; width: float32): Draw =
  result = d
  result.cmds.add "B " & dnum(x) & " " & dnum(y) & " " & dnum(w) & " " & dnum(h) &
                 " " & dnum(radius) & " " & $argb & " " & dnum(width) & ";"

proc traceArcPoints(d: Draw; cx, cy, r, startDeg, sweepDeg: float32; moveFirst: bool): Draw =
  ## Safe arc builder: avoids Skia's `arcTo` >= 180 deg and missing-current-point traps.
  result = d
  let steps = max(12, int(abs(sweepDeg) / 6.0'f32) + 1)
  for k in 0 .. steps:
    let deg = startDeg + sweepDeg * (float32(k) / float32(steps))
    let rad = deg * (PI / 180.0'f32)
    let px = cx + cos(rad) * r
    let py = cy + sin(rad) * r
    if k == 0 and moveFirst:
      result = result.moveTo(px, py)
    else:
      result = result.lineTo(px, py)

proc strokeArc(d: Draw; cx, cy, r, startDeg, sweepDeg: float32; col: uint32; width: float32): Draw =
  result = traceArcPoints(d, cx, cy, r, startDeg, sweepDeg, true).stroke(col, width)

proc fillArcChord(d: Draw; cx, cy, r, startDeg, sweepDeg: float32; col: uint32): Draw =
  result = traceArcPoints(d, cx, cy, r, startDeg, sweepDeg, true).close().fill(col)

proc strokeCircle(d: Draw; cx, cy, r: float32; col: uint32; width: float32): Draw =
  rectStroke(d, cx - r, cy - r, r * 2.0'f32, r * 2.0'f32, r, col, width)

proc sampleStops(stops: openArray[Stop]; t: float32): uint32 =
  if t <= stops[0].pos: return stops[0].col
  if t >= stops[^1].pos: return stops[^1].col
  var i = 0
  while i + 1 < stops.len:
    if t <= stops[i + 1].pos:
      let span = stops[i + 1].pos - stops[i].pos
      return mix(stops[i].col, stops[i + 1].col,
                 (if span <= 0.0'f32: 0.0'f32 else: (t - stops[i].pos) / span))
    inc i
  stops[^1].col

proc rampV(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h: float32; stops: openArray[Stop]; n = 48) =
  if h <= 0.0'f32 or w <= 0.0'f32: return
  for i in 0 ..< n:
    let f0 = float32(i) / float32(n)
    let f1 = float32(i + 1) / float32(n)
    d = d.rect(x, y + h * f0, w, h * (f1 - f0) + 0.6'f32,
               sampleStops(stops, (f0 + f1) * 0.5'f32))

proc rampH(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h: float32; stops: openArray[Stop]; n = 28) =
  if h <= 0.0'f32 or w <= 0.0'f32: return
  for i in 0 ..< n:
    let f0 = float32(i) / float32(n)
    let f1 = float32(i + 1) / float32(n)
    d = d.rect(x + w * f0, y, w * (f1 - f0) + 0.6'f32, h,
               sampleStops(stops, (f0 + f1) * 0.5'f32))

proc rampDisc(d: var Draw; cx, cy, r: float32; stops: openArray[Stop]; n = 24) =
  var i = n
  while i > 0:
    let f = float32(i) / float32(n)
    d = d.circle(cx, cy, r * f, sampleStops(stops, f))
    dec i

proc linDiag(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h: float32; stops: openArray[Stop]; n = 40) =
  if w <= 0.0'f32 or h <= 0.0'f32: return
  for i in 0 ..< n:
    let f0 = float32(i) / float32(n)
    let f1 = float32(i + 1) / float32(n)
    d = d.rect(x + w * f0, y + h * f0, w * (f1 - f0) + 1.0'f32,
               h * (f1 - f0) + 1.0'f32,
               sampleStops(stops, (f0 + f1) * 0.5'f32))

proc radialOverlayLayer(l: Layer; w, h: int; cx, cy, rad: float32; c0, c1: uint32) =
  ## Applies a true two-stop radial gradient over an existing Layer in-place
  ## without using `bDstOut` (which would erase underlying artwork).
  if w <= 0 or h <= 0 or rad <= 0.0'f32: return
  var buf = l.read(0, 0, w, h)
  if buf.len < w * h * 4: return
  let r0 = rgbOf(c0); let a0 = alphaOf(c0)
  let r1 = rgbOf(c1); let a1 = alphaOf(c1)
  let invR = 1.0'f32 / rad
  for y in 0 ..< h:
    let dy = float32(y) + 0.5'f32 - cy
    for x in 0 ..< w:
      let dx = float32(x) + 0.5'f32 - cx
      let t = cl(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * invR, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      let sa = a0 + (a1 - a0) * t
      if sa > 0.0005'f32:
        let sr = float32(r0[0]) + float32(r1[0] - r0[0]) * t
        let sg = float32(r0[1]) + float32(r1[1] - r0[1]) * t
        let sb = float32(r0[2]) + float32(r1[2] - r0[2]) * t
        let idx = (y * w + x) * 4
        let invA = 1.0'f32 - sa
        buf[idx]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sr * sa + float32(buf[idx]) * invA)))
        buf[idx + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sg * sa + float32(buf[idx + 1]) * invA)))
        buf[idx + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(sb * sa + float32(buf[idx + 2]) * invA)))
        buf[idx + 3] = 255'u8
  l.write(0, 0, w, h, buf)

proc grainPass(l: Layer; w, h: int; amt: float32; r: Rng) =
  if w <= 0 or h <= 0: return
  var buf = l.read(0, 0, w, h)
  if buf.len < w * h * 4: return
  var i = 0
  while i < w * h * 4:
    let delta = rnd32((r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * amt)
    buf[i]     = byte(clampByte(int32(buf[i]) + delta))
    buf[i + 1] = byte(clampByte(int32(buf[i + 1]) + delta))
    buf[i + 2] = byte(clampByte(int32(buf[i + 2]) + delta))
    i += 4
  l.write(0, 0, w, h, buf)

# ──────────────────────────────────────── 6. The Eight Generative Artworks

# --- Work I: Evening at Port Salin (Pointillist Sunset) ---
proc genSunset(d: var Draw; W, H: float32; r: Rng; n: Noise) =
  let hz = H * 0.57'f32
  let sx = W * 0.63'f32
  let sy = hz - H * 0.11'f32
  let sr = W * 0.052'f32

  rampV(d, 0, 0, W, hz, [
    (0.0'f32, hex(0x2F2D5E)), (0.42'f32, hex(0x8A5487)),
    (0.78'f32, hex(0xE3815A)), (1.0'f32, hex(0xF5BF6C))
  ], 40)
  rampV(d, 0, hz, W, H - hz, [
    (0.0'f32, hex(0xB86D6B)), (0.35'f32, hex(0x4F4776)),
    (1.0'f32, hex(0x1D2244))
  ], 24)

  proc landTop(x: float32): float32 =
    let t = x / W
    if t > 0.47'f32: return 1e9'f32
    hz - H * 0.21'f32 * pow(1.0'f32 - t / 0.47'f32, 1.25'f32) -
      H * 0.025'f32 * n.noiseAt(t * 7.0'f32, 1.3'f32)

  let tx0 = W * 0.115'f32
  let tx1 = W * 0.135'f32
  let tTop = landTop(W * 0.125'f32) - H * 0.13'f32
  let boatX = W * 0.34'f32
  let boatY = hz + H * 0.1'f32

  proc col(x, y: float32): (float32, float32, float32) =
    let lt = landTop(x)
    if x > tx0 and x < tx1 and y > tTop and y < lt + 3.0'f32:
      return (262.0'f32, 28.0'f32, 22.0'f32)
    let lampDx = x - (tx0 + tx1) * 0.5'f32
    let lampDy = y - (tTop - H * 0.014'f32)
    if sqrt(lampDx * lampDx + lampDy * lampDy) < W * 0.012'f32:
      return (52.0'f32, 100.0'f32, 84.0'f32)
    if y > lt and y < hz + H * 0.014'f32:
      return (275.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 30.0'f32, 30.0'f32, 15.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 9.0'f32)
    let bx = x - boatX
    let by = boatY - y
    if by > 0.0'f32 and by < H * 0.08'f32 and bx > -by * 0.55'f32 and bx < 2.0'f32:
      return (282.0'f32, 32.0'f32, 17.0'f32)
    if by > -H * 0.01'f32 and by <= 0.0'f32 and abs(bx + W * 0.005'f32) < W * 0.032'f32:
      return (282.0'f32, 32.0'f32, 14.0'f32)
    let ds = sqrt((x - sx) * (x - sx) + (y - sy) * (y - sy))
    if y < hz:
      if ds < sr: return (50.0'f32, 100.0'f32, 86.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 8.0'f32)
      let t = y / hz
      var h = if t < 0.45'f32: lerpF(245.0'f32, 305.0'f32, t / 0.45'f32)
              elif t < 0.8'f32: lerpF(305.0'f32, 370.0'f32, (t - 0.45'f32) / 0.35'f32)
              else: lerpF(10.0'f32, 44.0'f32, (t - 0.8'f32) / 0.2'f32)
      var l = lerpF(30.0'f32, 68.0'f32, t)
      var s = lerpF(45.0'f32, 82.0'f32, t)
      let glow = max(0.0'f32, 1.0'f32 - ds / (sr * 5.5'f32))
      l += glow * 22.0'f32
      h = lerpF(h, 45.0'f32, glow * 0.7'f32)
      if n.noiseAt(x / W * 3.0'f32, y / H * 15.0'f32) > 0.2'f32 and t > 0.25'f32 and t < 0.9'f32:
        h = 330.0'f32 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 24.0'f32
        l -= 9.0'f32
        s -= 8.0'f32
      return (h, s, l)
    let t = (y - hz) / (H - hz)
    var h = lerpF(250.0'f32, 236.0'f32, t)
    var l = lerpF(46.0'f32, 22.0'f32, t)
    var s = 44.0'f32
    let wv = sin(y * 0.45'f32 * (1.0'f32 + t) * (330.0'f32 / W) +
                 n.noiseAt(x * 0.02'f32 * (330.0'f32 / W), y * 0.05'f32) * 5.0'f32)
    l += wv * 5.0'f32
    let cw = W * (0.035'f32 + t * 0.12'f32) * (0.7'f32 + 0.3'f32 * n.noiseAt(y * 0.08'f32, 2.1'f32))
    if abs(x - sx) < cw and wv > -0.4'f32:
      h = 36.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 12.0'f32
      s = 92.0'f32
      l = lerpF(78.0'f32, 52.0'f32, t) + wv * 6.0'f32
    if t < 0.07'f32:
      h = lerpF(22.0'f32, h, t / 0.07'f32)
      l += (1.0'f32 - t / 0.07'f32) * 12.0'f32
    (h, s, l)

  let cnt = rnd32(W * H / 26.0'f32)
  let rad = W / 290.0'f32
  for _ in 0 ..< cnt:
    let x = r.nextF() * W
    let y = r.nextF() * H
    var (h, s, l) = col(x, y)
    h += (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 28.0'f32
    l += (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 14.0'f32
    let roll = r.nextF()
    if roll < 0.08'f32:
      h += 180.0'f32; s *= 0.8'f32
    elif roll < 0.2'f32:
      h += (if r.nextF() < 0.5'f32: -38.0'f32 else: 38.0'f32)
    let c = hsl(h, cl(s, 0.0'f32, 100.0'f32) / 100.0'f32, cl(l, 4.0'f32, 95.0'f32) / 100.0'f32)
    d = d.circle(x, y, rad * (0.75'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.7'f32), c)

  for _ in 0 ..< 420:
    let a = r.nextF() * 6.2832'f32
    let rr = sqrt(r.nextF()) * sr * 1.05'f32
    let c = hsl(46.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
                (80.0'f32 + r.nextF() * 14.0'f32) / 100.0'f32)
    d = d.circle(sx + cos(a) * rr, sy + sin(a) * rr, rad * 0.8'f32, c)

# --- Work II: Composition for a Quiet Street (Primary Grid) ---
type GridRect = object
  x, y, w, h: float32

proc genGrid(d: var Draw; W, H: float32; r: Rng) =
  d = d.rect(0, 0, W, H, hex(0xEFEADF))
  var leaves: seq[GridRect] = @[]
  var lines: seq[array[4, float32]] = @[]
  let minS = W * 0.13'f32

  proc split(rr: GridRect; depth: int32) =
    let canV = rr.w > minS * 2.0'f32
    let canH = rr.h > minS * 2.0'f32
    if depth > 4 or (not canV and not canH) or (depth > 1 and r.nextF() < 0.2'f32):
      leaves.add rr
      return
    let vert = canV and ((not canH) or (if rr.w >= rr.h: r.nextF() < 0.7'f32 else: r.nextF() < 0.3'f32))
    let f = [0.3'f32, 0.38'f32, 0.5'f32, 0.62'f32, 0.7'f32][int(r.nextF() * 5.0'f32)]
    if vert:
      let x = rr.x + max(minS, min(rr.w - minS, rr.w * f))
      lines.add [x, rr.y, x, rr.y + rr.h]
      split(GridRect(x: rr.x, y: rr.y, w: x - rr.x, h: rr.h), depth + 1)
      split(GridRect(x: x, y: rr.y, w: rr.x + rr.w - x, h: rr.h), depth + 1)
    else:
      let y = rr.y + max(minS, min(rr.h - minS, rr.h * f))
      lines.add [rr.x, y, rr.x + rr.w, y]
      split(GridRect(x: rr.x, y: rr.y, w: rr.w, h: y - rr.y), depth + 1)
      split(GridRect(x: rr.x, y: y, w: rr.w, h: rr.y + rr.h - y), depth + 1)

  split(GridRect(x: 0, y: 0, w: W, h: H), 0)

  var ordIdx = newSeq[int32](leaves.len)
  for i in 0 ..< leaves.len: ordIdx[i] = int32(i)
  for i in 1 ..< ordIdx.len:
    let key = ordIdx[i]
    let keyArea = leaves[key].w * leaves[key].h
    var j = i - 1
    while j >= 0 and (leaves[ordIdx[j]].w * leaves[ordIdx[j]].h) < keyArea:
      ordIdx[j + 1] = ordIdx[j]
      dec j
    ordIdx[j + 1] = key

  let n = leaves.len
  var cols = newSeq[uint32](n)
  const whites = [hex(0xEFEADF), hex(0xEBE5D8), hex(0xF2EEE4)]
  for o in ordIdx:
    cols[o] = whites[int(r.nextF() * 3.0'f32)]
  cols[ordIdx[0]] = hex(0xCF2A1F)
  let midLo = 2
  let midHi = max(3, int(float32(n) * 0.5'f32))
  let smLo = int(float32(n) * 0.5'f32)
  if midHi > midLo:
    cols[ordIdx[midLo + int(r.nextF() * float32(midHi - midLo))]] = hex(0x1F3F96)
  if n > smLo:
    cols[ordIdx[smLo + int(r.nextF() * float32(n - smLo))]] = hex(0xF0C02C)
    if n - smLo > 2:
      cols[ordIdx[smLo + int(r.nextF() * float32(n - smLo))]] =
        (if r.nextF() < 0.5'f32: hex(0xF0C02C) else: hex(0x161616))

  for o in ordIdx:
    let lf = leaves[o]
    d = d.rect(lf.x, lf.y, lf.w, lf.h, cols[o])
    d = d.save().clip(lf.x, lf.y, lf.w, lf.h)
    let horiz = lf.w > lf.h
    for _ in 0 ..< 70:
      let ga = 0.05'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.06'f32
      let white = r.nextF() < 0.5'f32
      let col = withA((if white: 0xFFFFFFFF'u32 else: 0xFF000000'u32), ga)
      if horiz:
        d = d.rect(lf.x + r.nextF() * lf.w * 0.3'f32 - lf.w * 0.1'f32,
                   lf.y + r.nextF() * lf.h,
                   lf.w * (0.4'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.7'f32),
                   0.6'f32 + r.nextF() * 1.6'f32, col)
      else:
        d = d.rect(lf.x + r.nextF() * lf.w,
                   lf.y + r.nextF() * lf.h * 0.3'f32 - lf.h * 0.1'f32,
                   0.6'f32 + r.nextF() * 1.6'f32,
                   lf.h * (0.4'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.7'f32), col)
    d = d.restore()

  for ln in lines:
    d = d.moveTo(ln[0], ln[1]).lineTo(ln[2], ln[3]).stroke(hex(0x141414), W * 0.024'f32)

# --- Work III: Ember Over Plum (Rothko Colour Field) ---
proc genColorField(d: var Draw; W, H: float32; r: Rng; n: Noise) =
  d = d.rect(0, 0, W, H, hex(0x3A0F18))
  for _ in 0 ..< 260:
    let c = if r.nextF() < 0.5'f32: rgba(96, 22, 34, 0.06'f32) else: rgba(18, 3, 8, 0.07'f32)
    d = d.rect(0, r.nextF() * H, W, r.nextF() * H * 0.07'f32, c)

  const
    bY0: array[3, float32] = [0.055'f32, 0.57'f32, 0.66'f32]
    bY1: array[3, float32] = [0.52'f32, 0.612'f32, 0.945'f32]
    bA: array[3, array[3, int32]] = [[210'i32, 62, 38], [36'i32, 8, 16], [86'i32, 28, 64]]
    bB: array[3, array[3, int32]] = [[238'i32, 124, 60], [60'i32, 16, 27], [124'i32, 50, 88]]
  let x0 = W * 0.08'f32
  let x1 = W * 0.92'f32
  let jw = W * 0.014'f32

  for bi in 0 .. 2:
    let Y0 = H * bY0[bi]
    let Y1 = H * bY1[bi]
    for _ in 0 ..< 48:
      let t = r.nextF()
      var c: array[3, int32]
      for ci in 0 .. 2:
        c[ci] = rnd32(float32(bA[bi][ci]) + float32(bB[bi][ci] - bA[bi][ci]) * t)
      let a0 = x0 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * jw * 2.0'f32
      let a1 = x1 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * jw * 2.0'f32
      let y0 = Y0 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * jw * 2.0'f32
      let y1 = Y1 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * jw * 2.0'f32
      d = d.rect(a0, y0, a1 - a0, y1 - y0, rgba(c[0], c[1], c[2], 0.075'f32))
    for _ in 0 ..< 280:
      let y = Y0 + r.nextF() * (Y1 - Y0)
      let t = r.nextF()
      var c: array[3, int32]
      for ci in 0 .. 2:
        c[ci] = min(255'i32, rnd32(float32(bB[bi][ci]) * (0.8'f32 + t * 0.45'f32)))
      let al = float32(rnd32((0.035'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.05'f32) * 1000.0'f32)) / 1000.0'f32
      let lw = 1.0'f32 + r.nextF() * W * 0.012'f32
      let xs = x0 + r.nextF() * W * 0.08'f32
      let ln = W * (0.2'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.62'f32)
      var s = 0.0'f32
      d = d.moveTo(xs, y)
      while s < ln:
        d = d.lineTo(xs + s, y + n.noiseAt((xs + s) * 0.012'f32, y * 0.012'f32) * H * 0.012'f32)
        s += W * 0.02'f32
      d = d.stroke(rgba(c[0], c[1], c[2], al), lw)

  rampDisc(d, W * 0.5'f32, H * 0.28'f32, W * 0.6'f32, [
    (0.0'f32, rgba(255, 176, 96, 0.022'f32)),
    (1.0'f32, rgba(255, 176, 96, 0.0'f32))
  ], 18)

# --- Work IV: Swell (Supersampled Op-Art Checkerboard) ---
proc genOp(W, H: int32; r: Rng; l: Layer) =
  let wf = float32(W)
  let hf = float32(H)
  let cs = wf / 15.0'f32
  let cx = wf * (0.56'f32 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 0.05'f32)
  let cy = hf * (0.44'f32 + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 0.05'f32)
  let inv = 1.0'f32 / (wf * 0.43'f32)
  const
    A0 = 242.0'f32
    A1 = 238.0'f32
    A2 = 229.0'f32
    B0 = 17.0'f32
    B1 = 16.0'f32
    B2 = 20.0'f32
  var buf = newSeq[byte](int(W) * int(H) * 4)
  for y in 0 ..< int(H):
    for x in 0 ..< int(W):
      var s = 0'i32
      for k in 0 .. 3:
        let px = float32(x) + (if (k and 1) != 0: 0.75'f32 else: 0.25'f32)
        let py = float32(y) + (if (k and 2) != 0: 0.75'f32 else: 0.25'f32)
        let dx = px - cx
        let dy = py - cy
        let r2 = (dx * dx + dy * dy) * inv * inv
        var m = 1.0'f32
        if r2 < 1.0'f32:
          let q = 1.0'f32 - r2
          m = 1.0'f32 - 0.56'f32 * q * q
        s += (int32(floor((cx + dx * m) / cs)) + int32(floor((cy + dy * m * 1.04'f32) / cs))) and 1
      let t = float32(s) * 0.25'f32
      let idx = (y * int(W) + x) * 4
      buf[idx]     = byte(clampByte(rnd32(A0 + (B0 - A0) * t)))
      buf[idx + 1] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(A1 + (B1 - A1) * t)))
      buf[idx + 2] = byte(clampByte(rnd32(A2 + (B2 - A2) * t)))
      buf[idx + 3] = 255'u8
  l.write(0, 0, int(W), int(H), buf)

# --- Work V: Mountains After Rain (Sumi-e Ink Landscape) ---
type InkPeak = object
  x, w, h: float32

proc inkAt(t, a: float32): uint32 {.inline.} =
  let v = rnd32(22.0'f32 + (1.0'f32 - t) * 52.0'f32)
  rgba(v, v, v + 8, a)

proc genInk(d: var Draw; W, H: float32; r: Rng; n: Noise) =
  d = d.rect(0, 0, W, H, hex(0xEFE6D2))
  rampV(d, 0, 0, W, H, [
    (0.0'f32, rgba(140, 100, 50, 0.04'f32)),
    (1.0'f32, rgba(140, 100, 50, 0.12'f32))
  ], 16)
  for _ in 0 ..< 90:
    let x = r.nextF() * W
    let y = r.nextF() * H
    d = d.moveTo(x, y).lineTo(
      x + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * W * 0.2'f32,
      y + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 6.0'f32
    ).stroke(rgba(120, 96, 60, 0.07'f32), 0.6'f32)

  const LAYERS = 5
  for ly in 0 ..< LAYERS:
    let t = float32(ly) / float32(LAYERS - 1)
    let base = H * (0.2'f32 + t * 0.34'f32)
    let amp = H * (0.09'f32 + t * 0.05'f32)
    let ln = H * (0.07'f32 + t * 0.09'f32)
    let a = 0.12'f32 + t * 0.74'f32
    var peaks: array[3, InkPeak]
    for k in 0 .. 2:
      peaks[k] = InkPeak(
        x: r.nextF() * W,
        w: W * (0.1'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.22'f32),
        h: 0.5'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.7'f32
      )
    proc ridge(x: float32): float32 =
      var v = 0.6'f32 * n.noiseAt(x / W * 2.4'f32 + float32(ly) * 3.7'f32, float32(ly) * 1.3'f32) +
              0.3'f32 * n.noiseAt(x / W * 7.0'f32 + float32(ly), 5.0'f32 + float32(ly))
      for p in peaks:
        let z = (x - p.x) / p.w
        v += p.h * exp(-(z * z))
      base - amp * v

    var x = 0.0'f32
    while x < W:
      let top = ridge(x) + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * 1.3'f32
      let L = ln * (0.65'f32 + 0.35'f32 * n.noiseAt(x * 0.03'f32, float32(ly) * 9.0'f32))
      rampV(d, x, top, 1.3'f32, L, [
        (0.0'f32, inkAt(t, a)),
        (0.18'f32, inkAt(t, a * 0.62'f32)),
        (1.0'f32, inkAt(t, 0.0'f32))
      ], 6)
      x += 1.0'f32

    if t > 0.3'f32:
      let cnt = int32(W * 0.35'f32 * t)
      for _ in 0 ..< cnt:
        let hx = r.nextF() * W
        let hy = ridge(hx) + r.nextF() * ln * 0.55'f32
        let ex = hx + W * (0.01'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.02'f32) *
                 (if r.nextF() < 0.5'f32: -1.0'f32 else: 1.0'f32)
        let ey = hy + H * (0.008'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.012'f32)
        d = d.moveTo(hx, hy).lineTo(ex, ey).stroke(inkAt(t, 0.28'f32 * t), W * 0.004'f32)

    rampV(d, 0, base + ln * 0.2'f32, W, ln * 1.1'f32, [
      (0.0'f32, rgba(239, 230, 210, 0.0'f32)),
      (0.5'f32, rgba(239, 230, 210, 0.7'f32)),
      (1.0'f32, rgba(239, 230, 210, 0.0'f32))
    ], 20)

  proc rockTop(x: float32): float32 =
    let z = (x - W * 0.18'f32) / (W * 0.3'f32)
    H * 0.8'f32 - H * 0.1'f32 * exp(-(z * z)) - H * 0.02'f32 * n.noiseAt(x * 0.02'f32, 40.0'f32)

  var rx = 0.0'f32
  while rx < W * 0.62'f32:
    let top = rockTop(rx)
    let L = H * 0.16'f32 * (1.0'f32 - rx / (W * 0.62'f32))
    if L > 0.0'f32:
      rampV(d, rx, top, 1.3'f32, L, [
        (0.0'f32, rgba(20, 20, 26, 0.92'f32)),
        (0.3'f32, rgba(20, 20, 26, 0.5'f32)),
        (1.0'f32, rgba(20, 20, 26, 0.0'f32))
      ], 8)
    rx += 1.0'f32

  proc pine(dr: var Draw; px, py, h, lean: float32) =
    dr = dr.moveTo(px, py).quadTo(px + lean * 0.6'f32, py - h * 0.5'f32, px + lean, py - h)
           .stroke(rgba(16, 16, 20, 0.9'f32), W * 0.012'f32)
    for k in 0 ..< 7:
      let f = 0.3'f32 + float32(k) * 0.1'f32
      let bx = px + lean * f * f
      let by = py - h * f
      let side = if k mod 2 == 1: 1.0'f32 else: -1.0'f32
      let span = W * (0.1'f32 - float32(k) * 0.009'f32)
      for s in 0 ..< 16:
        let nx = bx + side * span * (float32(s) / 16.0'f32)
        let ny = by - sin(float32(s) / 16.0'f32 * 3.1'f32) * H * 0.006'f32
        let al = float32(rnd32((0.55'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.35'f32) * 100.0'f32)) / 100.0'f32
        let c = rgba(16, 16, 20, al)
        let ex1 = nx + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * W * 0.025'f32
        let ey1 = ny - H * (0.006'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.008'f32)
        dr = dr.moveTo(nx, ny).lineTo(ex1, ey1).stroke(c, W * 0.004'f32)
        let ex2 = nx + (r.nextF() - 0.5'f32) * W * 0.025'f32
        let ey2 = ny + H * (0.004'f32 + r.nextF() * 0.006'f32)
        dr = dr.moveTo(nx, ny).lineTo(ex2, ey2).stroke(c, W * 0.004'f32)

  pine(d, W * 0.2'f32, rockTop(W * 0.2'f32) + 2.0'f32, H * 0.22'f32, W * 0.06'f32)
  pine(d, W * 0.34'f32, rockTop(W * 0.34'f32) + 2.0'f32, H * 0.14'f32, -W * 0.03'f32)

  for b in 0 ..< 3:
    let bx = W * (0.62'f32 + float32(b) * 0.07'f32)
    let by = H * (0.1'f32 + float32(b) * 0.018'f32)
    let s = W * 0.022'f32
    d = d.moveTo(bx - s, by - s * 0.2'f32)
         .quadTo(bx - s * 0.4'f32, by - s * 0.5'f32, bx, by)
         .quadTo(bx + s * 0.4'f32, by - s * 0.5'f32, bx + s, by - s * 0.25'f32)
         .stroke(rgba(20, 20, 26, 0.8'f32), W * 0.005'f32)

  let sz = W * 0.1'f32
  let sxp = W * 0.78'f32
  let syp = H * 0.9'f32
  d = d.rect(sxp, syp, sz, sz * 1.1'f32, hex(0xB3302A))
  d = d.rectStroke(sxp + sz * 0.16'f32, syp + sz * 0.16'f32, sz * 0.68'f32, sz * 0.78'f32,
                   0.0'f32, rgba(245, 230, 210, 0.85'f32), sz * 0.07'f32)
  d = d.moveTo(sxp + sz * 0.5'f32, syp + sz * 0.22'f32).lineTo(sxp + sz * 0.5'f32, syp + sz * 0.88'f32)
       .moveTo(sxp + sz * 0.24'f32, syp + sz * 0.55'f32).lineTo(sxp + sz * 0.76'f32, syp + sz * 0.55'f32)
       .stroke(rgba(245, 230, 210, 0.85'f32), sz * 0.07'f32)

# --- Work VI: Counterpoint No. 7 (Bauhaus Geometric Abstraction) ---
proc genGeometric(d: var Draw; W, H: float32; r: Rng) =
